## 3. Devil Woman and Monsters in Halloween Night Mayhem

▶ **[Watch video](https://cdn.lipsync.video/lipsync/cdn/feature-case/c9d7560d-30e5-40d2-9bc1-e9d15f5c2fc9/generate.mp4)**

<details>
<summary>Show full prompt</summary>

```text
STYLE: Photoreal raw flash footage, like a found Halloween night tape. Shot on a cheap early-2000s consumer camcorder with a fisheye lens: strong barrel distortion, harsh on-camera flash/light blasting whatever is close to the lens with blown-out highlights and hard shadows, everything past a few meters falls off into an underexposed, near-black background. 

Heavy camcorder noise, video grain, slight interlacing, smeared motion blur, auto-exposure pumping, occasional focus hunting, slightly crushed and green-tinted digital color. Chaotic documentary realism: the operator is a person running and reacting in real time, frantic handheld whip-pans, crash zooms, the camera bumping into things, subjects shoved right up into the fisheye lens. Meme energy: absurd moments played totally real. 

FAST pace: hard jump cuts every 0.5-1 second, no transitions. No on-screen text, no timestamp overlay, no titles, no logos, no watermark. CAST: @image (15) the devil woman. 

Dale the werewolf: a huge, hulking, shaggy grey-brown werewolf with glowing yellow eyes, a long snarling snout full of teeth, and matted fur. He wears a torn red varsity-style bowling jacket hanging off his massive frame, shredded at the sleeves. The skeletons: a trio of full-body human skeletons with bleached off-white bones, wearing mirrored aviator sunglasses and black tactical vests. One of them wears a bright orange baseball cap and white sneakers. The elderly witch: a frail, wrinkled old woman with wild grey hair, wearing a black feathered cape and sharp cat-eye glasses, with a pointed witch's hat and a crooked grin. The Headless Horseman: a tall rider in a burgundy velvet coat with no head, seated on a powerful black horse with a flowing mane. Shot 1 (0-2.2s): Flash-lit fisheye shot at a gas station at night, the devil woman bored against an old pickup, camera close on her face; out of the black behind her, the werewolf sprints into the flash and leaps straight at the lens; she swings up a shotgun and fires, muzzle flash whiting out the frame, camera knocked sideways. Shot 2 (2.2-2.8s): Whip-pan into a dark alley: three skeletons on a low wall lit only by the flash, one raises his rifle and they jump down at the camera. Shot 3 (2.8-3.6s): The devil woman leaps out of a black archway right past the lens, her red tulle skirt and sheer sleeves flaring and filling the frame, firing a rifle. Shot 4 (3.6-4s): Flash-blown close-up of muzzle flash and sparks, camera shaking. Shot 5 (4-5s): Running handheld through a dark farmyard toward a shed with propane tanks; the elderly witch zooms past the lens on a ride-on lawnmower, cape flapping, straight into the tanks. 

Shot 6 (5-6s): Massive fireball overexposes the camera; the witch is blasted up into the black sky; operator falls back. Shot 7 (6-6.6s): Pitch black cornfield, flash pops on: her broom stabs into the dirt right in front of the lens. Shot 8 (6.6-7.8s): 

City street at night, camera on the sidewalk; the Headless Horseman gallops inches past the fisheye lens holding a flaming jack-o'-lantern, fire flaring the image. Shot 9 (7.8-8.8s): 

A parked old sedan explodes, camera recoils, image clips to white then auto-exposes back. Shot 10 (8.8-12.2s): Packed barn Halloween party, flash on, crowd in witch hats and bedsheet ghosts screaming and holding up phones. 

Dance battle filmed from the front row: the devil woman, in her full devil costume with horns and heels, hits a sharp freeze right in the fisheye lens; the skeleton in the orange cap and white sneakers does a headspin, then collapses into a pile of loose bones. 

Crowd goes insane, camera shaking. Shot 11 (12.2-14s): Camera drops to the floor: harsh flash on scattered bones, the skull rolls straight into the lens and stops, distorted by the fisheye, background black. Hold. AUDIO: Raw camcorder mic audio, clipping and distorted: wind, werewolf roar, shotgun blast, people yelling 'YO!', lawnmower engine, explosions blowing out the mic, hooves, party crowd screaming, bones clattering, skull rolling. No music, no score.

## 4. A Tiny Woman’s Bathroom Survival Escape

▶ **[Watch video](https://cdn.lipsync.video/lipsync/cdn/feature-case/4a0e7ad6-2497-4e77-adff-8150347e325f/generate.mp4)**

**16:9 · 1080p · 30s · Seedance 2.5**

[Original post](https://x.com/AIwithkhan/status/2110699529041310046) · 2026-10-15 19:49 (UTC+08:00)

<details>
<summary>Show full prompt</summary>

```text
Create a 30-second, photorealistic live-action miniature-person VFX comedy video showing an ordinary shower turning into an extreme survival adventure. Use cinematic macro photography, ultra-shallow depth of field, realistic water physics, dynamic handheld camera movements, dramatic lighting, and spectacular scale contrast. The video should feel like a premium Hollywood miniature-action sequence filmed inside a real bathroom, with hilarious near-disasters and visually satisfying moments. Ultra-detailed 4K quality, realistic textures, natural motion blur, and physically believable interactions.

CHARACTER CONSISTENCY

The main character is a tiny adult woman approximately 1:5 human scale, with realistic facial features, expressive eyes, shoulder-length dark hair tied in a small ponytail, a pastel-blue sleeveless top, dark shorts, and tiny white sneakers. Keep her face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and scale perfectly consistent throughout the entire video. She must look like a real miniature person, not an animated or cartoon character.

SCENE SEQUENCE

Scene 1 — The Danger Begins
A tiny woman stands on the edge of a bathroom floor beside a giant shower area, confidently preparing for a shower. The camera slowly pushes in as she looks up at the enormous showerhead above her. Suddenly, the shower turns on, and her confident expression changes to pure terror.

Scene 2 — The First Waterfall
A massive stream of water crashes down like a powerful waterfall. She runs for cover as giant droplets explode against the tiles around her. One enormous droplet splashes beside her, sending her tumbling backward across the wet floor.

Scene 3 — Soap Bar Avalanche
She climbs onto a giant soap bar to escape the rushing water. The slippery soap suddenly slides across the floor like a runaway skateboard. She desperately balances on top, waving her arms while racing toward the drain.

Scene 4 — The Shampoo Bottle Collapse
She jumps off the soap bar and grabs the edge of a giant shampoo bottle for safety. The bottle wobbles, tips over, and releases a thick stream of shampoo onto the floor. She slides down the slippery shampoo trail like a water slide, barely avoiding a giant puddle.

Scene 5 — The Drain Escape
The water begins flowing rapidly toward the bathroom drain. She runs in the opposite direction as tiny waves chase her feet. At the last moment, she leaps onto a raised tile edge and grabs a small grout line, dangling dramatically above the rushing water.

Scene 6 — The Final Victory
She pulls herself onto a dry bathmat, exhausted but triumphant. Behind her, the shower suddenly releases one final giant splash that sends her flying forward onto the soft mat. She slowly lifts her head, gives the showerhead an angry look, and shakes her fist at it. End with a dramatic close-up of her soaked, furious face.

CAMERA AND VISUAL STYLE

- Cinematic macro close-ups emphasizing the tiny woman's scale against oversized bathroom objects.
- Low-angle tracking shots following her frantic escape.
- Fast whip pans, subtle handheld shake, and brief slow motion during major splashes and jumps.
- Realistic water droplets, splashes, wet surfaces, reflections, and slippery soap physics.
- Sharp focus on the character with beautiful background bokeh.
- Comedic timing, expressive facial reactions, and seamless transitions between action beats.
- Maintain consistent bathroom layout, lighting, character appearance, and object positions.

SOUND DESIGN

Use synchronized, exaggerated but realistic sound effects: shower valve click, thunderous rushing water, heavy splashes, slippery soap squeaks, shampoo bottle thud, wet sliding sounds, frantic footsteps, dramatic whooshes, and a final soft bathmat landing. Add playful, suspenseful instrumental background music that builds during the chase and ends with a funny musical sting.
```

</details>

A dark-fantasy warrior with long disheveled black hair, cold expression, tattered gray-white scarf, shattered dark cloak and white-gray outfit awakens an abandoned battlefield.

0–5s: A tiny white-blue spark triggers the entire environment. Stones vibrate, cracks spread, invisible pressure bends the air, and a colossal thunderstorm ring forms overhead.

20–27s: He launches into the fractured dimension, moving through floating ruins with punches, elbows and aerial strikes. All accumulated energy converges into a tiny, hyper-dense white-blue thunderstorm sphere.

27–30s: Absolute silence. One spark. His eyes open. He unleashes the WORLD-SPLITTING STRIKE—a colossal white-blue shockwave tears through reality, followed by a black-and-white spatial implosion.

## 7. Woman Outmaneuvers Three Thugs Outside a Convenience Store at Night

▶ **[Watch video](https://cdn.lipsync.video/lipsync/cdn/feature-case/0e69b938-5533-46ae-beab-c36b484342cf/generate.mp4)**

**16:9 · 720p · 30s · Seedance 2.5**

[Original post](https://x.com/aiwithaly/status/2106981702677451248) · 2026-10-05 13:35 (UTC+08:00)

<details>
<summary>Show full prompt</summary>

```text
Create an exactly 30-second, 16:9, 24fps hyper-realistic Korean live-action action thriller with dry dark comedy. Nighttime outside a modern Korean convenience store, wet pavement reflecting storefront lights.

PYONA exits carrying a small paper bag. Three intimidating adult men surround her. The leader says, “You have three choices: give us your phone, apologize, or fight.”

PYONA calmly studies the gap between them and says, “I'll take number four.” She walks through the opening and adds, “You leave.” The leader's smile disappears.

He grabs her forearm. She looks at his hand and says, “That was the wrong answer.” When he pulls her, she steps toward him, rotates with his force and redirects his momentum into the convenience-store door.

A second man charges with a punch. PYONA sidesteps and redirects him into the first man. A third attacks from behind; she steps aside and redirects him into stacked plastic crates, which collapse from the collision.

The three men freeze. The leader asks, “Who are you?” PYONA calmly replies, “I already told you.” She picks up her bag, walks past them, then looks back and says, “Number four.”

Cut to black.

Use grounded physical choreography: realistic weight, momentum, inertia, friction, leverage, balance and collision reactions. PYONA wins through timing and redirection, never superhuman strength.

Controlled cinematic camera with consistent screen direction and clear full-body framing during physical actions. Natural nighttime ambience and realistic impact sounds only. No music or narration.

Negative prompt: supernatural strength, flying bodies, wire-fu, invisible force, impossible throws, premature falls, teleportation, duplicate characters, appearance drift, costume changes, location changes, excessive camera shake, exaggerated slow motion, cartoon physics, blood, gore, weapons, broken bones, head impacts, logos, watermark.
```

</details>

SHOT 11 (40–44): Wide low-angle pull-back. Hero stands calmly between all three fallen attackers as rain pours over his armor and crimson seams gradually dim.

SHOT 12 (44–48): Extreme close-up of the hero lifting his head with quiet confidence. A tiny red spark crosses his eyes before the image cuts to black.

One single continuous 20-second shot, 16:9 landscape, 1080p. NO cuts, NO edits, NO transitions, NO fades, NO title card, NO slow-motion. One unbroken handheld-gimbal camera that moves with the action the entire time. Photoreal supernatural school drama.

STORY
A quiet girl is cornered by four classmates in an old shrine courtyard behind her school. They tear out the pressed-flower pages she has been collecting in her journal and shove her down. Her hurt turns to resolve, and the wind itself answers her. She defeats the four one at a time with acrobatic moves that create visible gusts, calls her flying pages back out of the air, and walks away with them.

REFERENCES
@image1 = the heroine. One girl shown in two views (full body and face close-up); both views are the SAME person. Keep her exact face, dark teal-black layered shoulder-length hair with side-swept bangs, silver hairclip, brown eyes, small beauty mark, slim build.
@image2 = four antagonists, mapped left to right: A = tall, long straight black hair, center part (the leader); B = chestnut bob with blunt bangs; C = high black ponytail with white scrunchie; D = wavy dark-brown hair past the shoulders. Keep each face and hairstyle independent and consistent; never swap identities.
All five wear the reference uniform exactly: navy double-button blazer with white lapel trim, white shirt, red ribbon tie, plaid pleated knee-length skirt, black tights, brown loafers. No other clothing. Do NOT show the white studio backgrounds, lineup poses or split-screen layouts from the references. All five people are the same real-world scale.

NO DISAPPEARING (CRITICAL)
Exactly five real people exist in this scene from the first frame to the last frame: the heroine, A, B, C and D. NOBODY vanishes, fades, dissolves, turns into leaves or dust, teleports, gets blown out of the scene, or is swallowed by the wind. Wind never removes a person from the shot. When the camera moves away from a character and returns, she is still exactly where she was left. Every defeated girl stays physically on the ground in her defeated position, visible, solid and breathing, until the very last frame. Nobody walks or runs out of the courtyard. The count of people stays at five at all times.

SETTING
An old shrine courtyard behind a high school, late-afternoon golden light, soft overcast sky. Gravel and stone-slab ground, two moss-covered stone lanterns, a small wooden shrine gate (torii) in the background, a large ginkgo tree dropping yellow leaves. A low wooden bench on the left. Everything is flat and open, no stairs, no drops, no water. The wind is NOT a storm: it is a controlled, localized swirl of a limited number of yellow ginkgo leaves, light gravel dust and moving hair. The leaves stay thin and sparse, never form a thick cloud or wall, and never cover a person's body or face. Wind is transparent: no glowing beams, no neon, no smoke, no animal shapes, no tornado.

PROPS
One small hand-bound journal with a green cloth cover. Exactly five thin washi-paper pages, each holding a pressed flower (a pink cosmos, a blue hydrangea, a red maple leaf, a tiny forget-me-not, a white camellia), are torn out together. The journal is dropped near the heroine's feet and stays there until she picks it up. The five pages float in the air at the left side of the courtyard during the fight and never act as weapons. Two school bags rest against the shrine wall from the start and are never touched.

CAMERA, ONE CONTINUOUS MOVE
The camera starts in a tight handheld medium shot on the heroine's face, drifts around her, then follows each action in a smooth flowing path at eye level and side three-quarter angles, mostly medium to medium-full so every acrobatic move is fully visible. It never cuts. It sweeps from character to character with whip-pan-style swings that settle on the next target. After each defeat, the camera glides briefly across the fallen girl so the audience sees she is still there. At the climax the camera does one 100-degree orbit around the heroine at normal speed. In the final seconds the camera slowly pulls back to a medium-wide view that shows all five people.

PERFORMANCE TIMELINE (all in one take)

0–4s: Camera starts close on the heroine, holding her green journal against her chest. A (long black hair) snatches it, holds it out of reach and says in a mocking voice, in English: "This is trash." She tears five pressed-flower pages out in one handful and flings them into the air while the others laugh. Camera pans with the pages fluttering, then returns.

4–7s: She turns to chase the pages. B (chestnut bob) steps in and shoves her; she stumbles, drops to one knee and one palm, the journal falls near her. A few loose ginkgo leaves skitter across the gravel. Camera pushes in on her wet eyes and tight jaw. Laughter fades. Hold the hurt for a beat. A, B, C and D are all visible standing nearby.

7–9s: She says quietly and firmly, in English: "Stop it already." Her hair lifts, a few leaves begin to circle, and the five pages stop and hover on the left. The four step back, uneasy, all clearly visible. B moves in first.

9–12s: DEFEAT 1, B. The heroine pushes off her palm into a single handspring into a side cartwheel. Her spinning legs pull the air into one narrow gust that travels sideways across the gravel into B's chest. B is thrown off balance, slides, and ends seated against the left stone lantern base. She stays there, visible, for the rest of the video.

12–16s: DEFEAT 2, C. The camera glances at B still seated, then whips to C, who charges from the right. The heroine drops into a low crouch and slides on the gravel into one long circular leg sweep. The sweep sends a flat current of leaves and dust along the ground into C's ankles. C's feet slip and she falls forward onto her hands and knees at the right edge. She stays there, visible.

16–20s: DEFEAT 3, D. D rushes in with both hands out. The heroine springs up, performs one standing back handspring into a backflip. As she rotates, her heels drag a wide, flat wall of air in front of her that hits D at torso height. D is pushed back two steps and sits down hard on the wooden bench. She stays seated, visible. Nobody collides with anyone else.

DEFEAT POSITIONS (never reset)
B: seated at the left stone lantern. C: hands and knees at the right edge. D: seated on the bench. A: seated alone near the torii gate. All four stay conscious, remain in the courtyard, never rejoin the fight and are never hit by another person's attack.

RULES FOR THE POWERS
Every technique follows: acrobatic movement, wind forms, wind travels, wind makes contact, reaction. The heroine's feet never touch anyone. Each gust fades after its own hit. Only the targeted person is affected; the others may flinch. No blades of air, no blood, no injuries, no one hits their head. One execution per move: no repeated flips, no extra spins.

AUDIO
Natural courtyard ambience, distant cicadas, rustling leaves, gravel scrapes, shoes, paper tearing, mocking laughter, breathing, cloth movement, wind whooshes, bodies landing on gravel. Only TWO spoken lines in the whole video, both in clear natural English with accurate lip sync: A says "This is trash." and the heroine says "Stop it already." No other dialogue. No music, no narrator, no subtitles, no on-screen text.

LOOK
Natural daylight color with warm golden tones, fine film grain, soft halation, real skin texture, no plastic faces, no HDR glow, no vignette, no letterboxing. Side-on, athletic stunt framing; clothing moves naturally with the acrobatics, never framed as spectacle.

AVOID

END

Create a raw kung fu performance storyboard focused on extreme physical action. Use reference image for the character.

Keep the artwork lightweight, dynamic and unfinished like early fight choreography previs.

Start directly in action. Do not begin with a calm stance, preparation shot or slow introduction.

A solitary female performer executes an aggressive Tibetan kung fu master-style routine inside a vast ancient temple. The choreography is exaggerated, explosive and constantly escalating: flying diagonal kicks, monk-style low stances, rapid palm strikes, spinning cloth-like body turns, animal-form hand shapes, deep lunges, aerial twists, floor-level sweeps, sudden drops, claw-like blocks, back-arched jumps, sliding recoveries and violent sculptural impact poses.

Every panel must contain visible motion and strong body momentum. Avoid static standing poses. The performer should feel like a ritual warrior moving with discipline, fury, spiritual pressure and total body control.

Action progression:
1. begin mid-air with a flying diagonal kick already in motion
2. handheld close-up palm sweep cutting through air
3. orbiting wide shot of a full-body spin
4. low-angle impact palm strike with shockwave
5. long-lens side profile spinning kick
6. top-down aerial turn with body, hair and fabric flaring outward
7. hard floor stomp cracking the temple stone
8. sliding low sweep across the floor
9. aggressive close-up flurry of elbows, palms and backfist strikes
10. extreme low monk-style beast stance with energy rising
11. spinning elemental vortex around the body
12. final airborne action pose, suspended above the temple floor, body twisted in a powerful kung fu strike, all elements converging around her before impact

Add selective elemental energy effects as VFX-style storyboard accents. The effects should feel spiritual, ritualistic and cinematic, not superhero-like:
air bursts around spins and flying kicks,
dust and stone fragments lifting from stomps,
water-like floor ripples during slides,
fire-like trails around explosive strikes,
heat distortion around high-intensity movement,
elemental vortex near the climax.

Element progression:
early panels: subtle wind, dust and pressure lines
middle panels: stronger stone fragments, floor ripples and air shockwaves
late panels: controlled fire trails and energy spirals
final panel: the strongest combined elemental surge while the performer is still airborne

Use cinematic arthouse action camerawork:
handheld energy,
whip-pan feeling,
orbiting camera moves,
overhead shots,
side silhouettes,
aggressive close-ups,
long-lens compression,
extreme low angles,
wide negative space,
strong parallax.

Keep the temple environment minimal and atmospheric:
towering stone columns,
worn temple floor,
drifting incense smoke,
hanging fabric,
harsh light shafts,
faint dust in the air,
subtle wet floor reflections.
Do not overcrowd the frames.

Annotation color system:
red arrows = body movement
blue arrows = camera movement
green marks = framing / composition notes
orange marks = lighting direction
yellow marks = elemental VFX / energy effects
black text = short lens notes and panel labels

No timestamps. No dialogue. No singing. No extra characters. No enemies. No logos. No watermark.


Seedance 2.0 Prompt:

Create a 15-second cinematic kung fu performance video.

Use @[image1]  as the fixed character sheet reference. The character must strictly match the character sheet.
Use @[image2]  as the storyboard reference.

Follow the storyboard shot by shot as the main source for action order, camera rhythm, body movement, framing, movement direction, camera angles and visual progression. Treat each storyboard panel as a sequential keyframe. Preserve the shot order and make the video feel like the storyboard has been translated into continuous live-action motion. The sequence must end on a frozen final frame while the performer is still airborne.

Do not add text, captions, storyboard labels, arrows, UI, logos or watermarks. Do not treat the storyboard as a single image. Do not redesign the character, change the costume or alter the face. Do not begin with a calm stance, preparation pose or slow introduction. Do not make the elemental effects look like superhero powers or excessive fantasy glow.

Visual style:
stylized cinematic realism, high-end 3D painterly animation quality, dynamic cloth simulation, expressive silhouette design, rich cinematic lighting, controlled color palette, natural motion blur, dramatic scale, beautiful but aggressive physicality, premium feature-animation aesthetic.

Environment:
vast ancient temple, towering stone columns, worn temple floor, drifting incense smoke, hanging fabric, harsh light shafts, faint dust in the air, subtle wet floor reflections, high contrast shadows.

The performance is a solitary female kung fu routine inside a vast ancient temple. The routine starts immediately in action, with no calm stance, no preparation pose and no slow introduction. The movement should feel aggressive, ritualistic, disciplined, physically extreme and spiritually charged.

This is not a fight against an enemy. It is a solo performance of force, control, exhaustion, fury and release.

Follow story board for choreography direction.

Element progression:
early sequence: subtle wind, dust and pressure lines responding to movement.
middle sequence: stronger air shockwaves, stone fragments, floor cracks and water-like ripples across the temple floor.
late sequence: controlled fire trails, heat distortion and energy spirals around explosive strikes and kicks.
climax: wind, dust, stone, water ripple and fire accents combine into a stronger elemental vortex.
final beat: the performer is airborne above the temple floor in a powerful kung fu strike, body twisted mid-air, hair and fabric flaring outward, with all elements converging around her before impact.

Elemental VFX must feel spiritual, ritualistic and cinematic. The effects should be integrated with the choreography and motivated by physical movement. Keep the energy raw, elemental, atmospheric and grounded in the temple environment.

Use Laban movement logic throughout:
weight: strong, heavy, grounded during impacts, with brief lightness during jumps and aerial twists
time: quick during strikes, kicks, drops and turns, sustained during suspended holds and recovery transitions
space: direct during attacks, blocks and lunges, indirect during spinning turns and elemental vortex moments
flow: bound during rooted stances and precise strikes, free during aerial motion, spinning fabric movement and elemental release

Urban Escape Scene

Models ( GPT Image-2 + Seedance 2.0)

Seedance Prompts 2/3

MOTION PHRASES:

P01: Continuation wide. Vex crouches at the rooftop edge, spotlight sweeping over her as police close in behind.

P02: She commits—launching across a rooftop gap in a full-extension jump, hair and bag whipping in the wind.

P03: Hard landing on a slick roof. She catches herself in a low slide through rainwater and instantly regains control.

P04: She redirects to a vertical pipe and fire-escape corner, using them to descend fast and fluidly.

P05: She drops onto a slanted lower roof and slides down it, controlling speed with one hand while police appear above in pursuit.

P06: Vex lands in a narrow alley at street level, absorbing the impact in a three-point landing as steam vents around her.

P07: She spots her parked sleek Japanese motorcycle ahead, turns, and sprints toward it while lights flare behind her.

P08: She swings onto the bike, starts it fast, and snaps into riding position as pursuing officers spill into the alley.

P09: Final getaway shot. Vex rockets forward on the motorcycle, spraying water from the rear tire and blasting out of the alley as police lights chase behind. End on her accelerating into the neon-lit street.

Seedance Prompts 3/3

MOTION PHRASES:

P01: Rear chase wide. Vex accelerates down the flooded neon street as a police cruiser gains behind her.

P02: Low front three-quarter shot. She leans aggressively through a sharp turn, rear tire sliding slightly while the cruiser struggles to follow.

P03: Vex cuts into a narrow market alley, threading between stacked crates and wet barriers with inches to spare.

P04: She hits a makeshift ramp and launches the motorcycle over a construction divider as the pursuing cruiser brakes below.

P05: Hard landing. The suspension compresses, the underside throws sparks, and Vex regains perfect control without slowing.

P06: She enters an even tighter service passage, folding low over the tank and slipping through a gap too narrow for the police car.

P07: The cruiser attempts the turn, clips the entrance barrier, and becomes blocked behind her. Sirens remain trapped in the distance.

P08: Wide waterfront road. Vex emerges onto an open elevated route, checks behind her, and sees no police vehicles remaining on her trail.

P09: Final victory shot. She accelerates toward the glowing skyline on the empty rain-slick road. The sirens fade completely as the camera holds on her taillight disappearing into the neon night.

# STORYBOARD PROMPT — VIPER vs ORC FIGHT CHOREOGRAPHY (v1)

Title block, top edge of sheet, clean hand-lettered style: "ROGUE VIPER — EP3 | VIPER vs ORC — FIGHT CHOREOGRAPHY | BOARD 1 of 1 | ARRI ALEXA 35". Panels numbered 1–12 in small ink numerals at the top-left corner of each panel frame.

5. (C — overhead) Top-down footwork map: she circles fast to his blind side in a wide arc while he pivots heavily on planted feet, club dragging. GOLD: long smooth orbit arc with three step-ticks. RED: short stubby rotation arc at his center.
6. (C — overhead) He hoists the club vertical for a two-handed overhead smash; she darts straight inside his stance. RED: coiled vertical wind-up spiral. GOLD: straight penetration dart to his center.
7. (D — over the orc's shoulder) The overhead smash detonates on empty floor past her; she is mid-sidestep, katana biting across his flank. RED: full-commitment vertical crush vector with impact star. GOLD: lateral sidestep hook ending in a flank slash arc.
8. (D — over the orc's shoulder) He staggers a half-step from the flank hit; she rebounds off the floor into a leaping strike toward his shoulder line. RED: broken stumble tick-marks. GOLD: steep launch vector, body extended, blade cocked.
9. (E — close lateral) Mid-air she meets a rising club sweep — she blocks on the flat of the katana and the impact hurls her backward. RED: rising uppercut arc with impact star at contact. GOLD: sharp deflection kink then a long recoil vector.
10. (A — wide profile) Full-width panel beat: she is airborne mid back-flip recovering, katana still gripped; the orc is already charging after her, club dragging a gouge line. GOLD: aerial flip loop with landing prediction dash. RED: heavy straight charge vector, low and accelerating.
11. (B — low angle) She lands in a deep skid, blade coming to horizontal guard as the orc's charge fills the frame, club winding into a full-body 180° swing. GOLD: skid friction ticks into a coiled ready spiral. RED: massive wind-up arc wrapping behind his shoulder mass.
12. (E — close lateral) FREEZE ON THE CLIFFHANGER: club and katana converging in mid-air, both fighters at full commitment, an instant before contact — the collision point left undrawn. RED and GOLD arcs converge on a single empty gap at frame center.

@ref_choreography — 12-panel fight board; blocking, motion paths, and camera setups only; rendered output is live-action photoreal footage.
@loc_arena — environment and architecture: circular white stone arena floor with radial seam lines, steep tiered stands, colonnaded upper gallery, open sky above the ring. 100% matches the reference for architecture; the stands are filled to capacity with spectators in every cut.
@char_RV_Viper — athletic female fighter, long black hair, black leather bodysuit with gold seam piping, mandarin collar, gold cobra belt buckle, thigh-high heeled boots, drop-leg twin holster harness. 100% matches the reference.
@char_RV_Orc — green-skinned bald orc gladiator with lower tusks, gunmetal-and-bronze plated armor, massive two-handed spiked club. Stands half a body taller than the swordswoman, three times her mass. 100% matches the reference.
@prop_katana — steel katana, black silk-wrapped hilt with gold diamond menuki, round engraved brass tsuba. 100% matches the reference.
@prop_pistols — matte black suppressed pistols seated in black leather drop-leg thigh holsters. 100% matches the reference.

LOCATION MAP
Foreground: polished white stone arena floor with radial seam lines and light scuff marks, a hard-edged pool of direct sunlight falling across the duel circle. Midground: the low white perimeter wall ringing the floor. Background: steep tiered stands packed shoulder-to-shoulder with spectators, rising to a colonnaded upper gallery; open daylight sky above the ring, haze at 80 meters depth softening the far side of the bowl. Sun sits high camera-left above the gallery rim. Operator holds the shadow side in every setup.

FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING
First frame opens mid-action: wide profile two-shot inside the sunlight pool — the orc frame-right halfway through a flat horizontal club swing at chest height, the swordswoman frame-left already dropping into a low knee-slide beneath the swing arc, katana drawn and trailing. Screen direction locked for the full clip: she attacks from frame-left, he holds frame-right.

FORMAT MODE
Timed multishot. Hard cuts at the stated seconds only; cuts only at the specified points, the camera does not cut on its own.

OPTICS
CUT 1: 63° wide profile. CUT 2: 84° low angle. CUT 3: 47° overhead. CUT 4: 47° over-the-shoulder. CUT 5: 29° lateral medium. CUT 6: 63° wide profile. Fixed FOV per segment, no drift mid-segment. 35mm anamorphic character, oval bokeh, fine grain.

CAMERA
Wide tonal latitude, soft highlight roll-off. CUT 1 and CUT 6 locked at chest height; CUT 2 locked at floor height angled up; CUT 3 directly overhead, static, the full duel circle and packed lower rows in frame; CUT 4 anchored behind the orc's right shoulder; CUT 5 tracking laterally at torso height, matching her speed.

ACTION
0.0s to 2.5s — Wide profile: the club head sweeps through at 60 km/h; she slides under it at 25 km/h; the club hammers the white floor where she was — cracks spider out along the radial seams, dust ring — as she shoulder-rolls left past his planted leg.
2.5s HARD CUT
2.5s to 5.0s — Low angle: she springs up inside his reach, rising diagonal slash across his armored forearm — sparks; he backhands one-armed with the club haft; she arches backward under it, blade sweeping overhead as counterweight; packed stands and open sky behind them.
5.0s HARD CUT
5.0s to 7.0s — Overhead: she circles fast to his blind side in a wide arc at 20 km/h while he pivots heavily on planted feet, then hoists the club vertical for a two-handed overhead smash; their shadows stretch across the sunlit white floor.
7.0s HARD CUT
7.0s to 9.5s — Over the orc's shoulder: the smash detonates on empty white floor — dust ring, floor cracks; she is mid-sidestep, katana biting across his flank plate with a spark trail; he staggers a half-step.
9.5s HARD CUT
9.5s to 12.0s — Lateral medium: she rebounds off the floor into a leaping strike toward his shoulder line; a rising club sweep meets her mid-air; she blocks on the flat of the katana and the impact hurls her backward across frame.
12.0s HARD CUT

PERFORMANCE
Her eyes stay locked on the orc through every exchange, jaw set, controlled sharp exhales; hair whips on each direction change. The orc grimaces around his tusks on the flank hit, chest heaving on the final charge.

The orc moves with visible mass — floor shudders on his steps, full-body follow-through on every swing, dust kicked from each footfall. She is light and sudden: sharp direction changes, boots skidding with friction marks on the polished stone. Sparks where steel meets armor plate; holster straps and hair react to inertia; grounded contact shadows under both fighters in the direct sun.

LIGHTING
Hard direct sunlight from high camera-left above the gallery rim, 5600K, throwing a bright pool across the duel circle; cool open-sky fill from above; the stands sit one stop down in bounced light; fighters carry crisp single sun shadows plus soft sky fill.

COLOR GRADE
Steel blue-grey palette, moderate desaturation, crushed blacks, retained midtones; the sunlit floor reads clean paper-white against cooler shaded stands; her gold seam piping and the brass tsuba catch the sun as the only warm accents.

AUDIO
Deep concussive club impacts, blade whoosh and metallic ring on armor contact, boot skids on polished stone, open-air crowd roar rising in waves that spike on each impact.

STYLE
Photoreal live-action, 35mm anamorphic film character, fine grain, bloodless stylized combat.

OUTPUT SETTINGS
16:9, all segments real-time speed.

Katana stays in her grip in all six cuts. Both pistols stay seated in the thigh holsters in every cut. She works frame-left, the orc holds frame-right, in every cut. ```text
Create a 60-second ultra-realistic cinematic Korean action-thriller scene featuring a young woman with straight black hair, a sharp, confident expression, and a distinctive outfit: a black oversized jacket, light-colored top, black pants, and a bright red scarf. She is inside a dimly lit classroom, facing an armed opponent. The scene combines intense gunfight choreography, acrobatic martial arts, dramatic close-ups, and stylish action-film cinematography.

SCENE 1 — THE INTRODUCTION (0–6 seconds)

Open with a medium close-up of the young woman standing motionless in a dark classroom. She looks directly ahead with a calm, intimidating expression. Her black hair frames her face, and her red scarf contrasts with her dark clothing.

Cut to an extreme close-up of her face as she slightly tilts her head and purses her lips, maintaining a cool, fearless attitude. Insert a brief close-up of a handgun resting on a nearby desk to establish the danger.

SCENE 2 — THE ATTACK BEGINS (6–12 seconds)

Cut to a wider classroom shot. An armed opponent suddenly approaches. The woman immediately lowers her body, shifts her weight, and prepares to evade.

Use a fast tracking shot as she moves forward, followed by a sudden camera cut to her opponent. The choreography should feel fast, fluid, and physically convincing, with realistic body mechanics and clear spatial continuity.

SCENE 3 — ACROBATIC FIGHT (12–22 seconds)

The woman launches into an explosive sequence of martial arts movements. She ducks beneath an attack, pivots, performs a high spinning kick, and uses rapid changes of direction to avoid her opponent.

Capture the action with a combination of low-angle shots, wide shots, and close tracking movements. Her jacket and red scarf move naturally with her momentum.

Show brief flashes of gunfire and bright muzzle flashes illuminating the dark classroom. The camera alternates between the woman's controlled movements and the opponent's reactions.

SCENE 4 — THE GUNFIGHT (22–36 seconds)

Escalate the confrontation with rapid exchanges of gunfire. The woman takes cover, changes position, and uses the classroom desks as obstacles.

Include dramatic low-angle shots of her moving across the room, quick close-ups of her focused eyes, and brief shots of the muzzle flashes lighting the windows and furniture.

At one point, use a dramatic ground-level perspective as she stands over the camera's viewpoint, holding her weapon with a determined expression.

Cut to a close-up of her hands holding a firearm, then return to a wider shot showing her movement through the classroom.

SCENE 5 — THE FINAL EXCHANGE (36–48 seconds)

The action becomes more intense. The woman moves sideways while maintaining awareness of her opponent. Show a fast sequence of evasive movements, sharp turns, and acrobatic kicks.

Use dynamic handheld cinematography, short whip pans, and occasional slow motion at the most dramatic moments. Include realistic motion blur without losing the clarity of the choreography.

The opponent attempts to regain control, but she continues moving confidently through the classroom.

SCENE 6 — THE CINEMATIC FINISH (48–60 seconds)

Slow the pacing briefly. Show the woman standing with a confident, almost playful expression. Her hair is slightly disheveled, and her red scarf hangs loosely around her neck.

Cut to a wide shot of the classroom as the confrontation resumes. The woman and her opponent exchange a final sequence of rapid movements, with one last dramatic dodge and counterattack.

End with a close-up of her face as she looks toward the camera with a subtle, confident expression. Finish with a final cinematic action shot and a sharp cut to black.

CINEMATOGRAPHY AND VISUAL STYLE

- Photorealistic Korean action-film cinematography.
- Cool blue-green color grading, dark shadows, and muted classroom colors.

IMPORTANT CONSISTENCY INSTRUCTIONS
```

```text
SCENE CONTEXT
An old wooden Japanese school corridor during festival-prep week. Yuki stands on a low wooden stepladder, tying paper crane streamers along an overhead beam. Three upperclassmen — led by Ren — approach from the corridor's far end and shove the ladder to startle her.

ACTIVE REFERENCES
@yuki: 17-year-old Japanese high school girl, black hair to shoulder blades, navy seifuku uniform, red neck tie, calm composed expression. 100% matches the reference.
@ren: 18-year-old Japanese upperclassman, short messy black hair, loosened uniform blazer, smug expression. 100% matches the reference. Two additional upperclassmen share his uniform and build, faces unremarkable and non-distinct.

LOCATION MAP
Foreground: worn wooden floorboards, paper crane streamers hanging overhead. Midground: Yuki on the stepladder, corridor stretching back toward sliding shoji-style windows. Background: soft-focus students passing at the far end of the hall. Warm light spills in from the windows on camera-left; camera holds on the shadow side.

FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING
Yuki stands mid-step on the ladder, one hand raised tying a streamer, weight balanced, facing the beam — not the camera. Ren and the two upperclassmen enter frame from the right background, walking casually.

FORMAT MODE
Timed multishot. Hard cuts only at the stated seconds; the camera does not cut on its own between them.

OPTICS
0:00–0:04 — 63° wide observational FOV. 0:04–0:09 — 47° neutral FOV, tightening to 29° on close reactions. 0:09–0:15 — 47° neutral FOV. No drift mid-segment.

CAMERA
0:00–0:02: Static low 63° wide shot from floor height, holding Yuki on the ladder mid-corridor.
0:02–0:04: Sharp handheld push-in as the ladder tips, camera drops slightly with her fall.
0:04–0:06: Rapid tracking shot alongside her cartwheel, whip-pan into a 29° close-up on her face as she lands.
0:06–0:09: 360-degree combat orbit around the group as she trades blows with two attackers.
0:09–0:12: Static 47° medium shot, holding the throw and final takedown.
0:12–0:15: Slow 47° push-in on Yuki's face as she finishes tying the streamer.

ACTION
0:00–0:02 — Yuki calmly loops a paper crane streamer around a nail on the beam, humming lightly, unaware of the group approaching.
0:02–0:04 — Ren kicks the base of the stepladder. It skids and tips sideways under real weight and momentum. Yuki loses footing but stays composed — she pushes off the falling ladder into a controlled aerial cartwheel, hair trailing with the rotation, and lands the rotation directly into a spinning back kick that catches the nearest attacker in the shoulder, pushing him back two steps. Slow-motion only through the cartwheel and kick transition, then snap to real-time on impact.
0:04–0:06 — She lands lightly, weight absorbed through bent knees. A second attacker lunges; Yuki sidesteps by centimeters, pivots, and drives a palm-block into his forearm, redirecting his momentum past her.
0:06–0:09 — Ren and the remaining attacker close in from both sides. Yuki ducks one swing, catches the other attacker's arm, and executes a hip throw that drops him to the floorboards with a controlled, realistic fall. She rotates to block Ren's incoming punch with a forearm cross-block.
0:09–0:12 — Yuki catches Ren's wrist mid-swing, steps into him, and uses his own momentum to sweep his leg out, dropping him to sit hard on the floor, winded but unharmed. All three attackers are down, sitting or kneeling, defeated without gore.
0:12–0:15 — Yuki exhales, straightens her uniform collar, and picks the fallen streamer up off the floor. She climbs back onto the ladder and resumes tying it to the beam as if nothing happened, glancing once over her shoulder at Ren with a small knowing smile. She says: "Do try to help next time, senpai."

PERFORMANCE
Yuki's expression stays unhurried throughout — no panic, no strain, breath steady even mid-fight. Micro-details: a small controlled inhale before the cartwheel, a relaxed jaw during the throw, genuine amusement (not mockery) in the closing smile.

PHYSICS
Full gravity and inertia: the ladder skids and tips before she leaves it, her landings absorb through the knees, thrown attackers hit the floor with real contact weight and a puff of dust from the boards. No floating, no weightless movement.

LIGHTING
Warm natural window light from camera-left at a low afternoon angle, soft haze in the corridor air. Camera stays on the shadow side throughout for contrast. White balance 4500K.

COLOR GRADE
Warm wood tones and cream uniform whites as the dominant palette, deep navy from the uniforms as the secondary, and the red of Yuki's neck tie as the only accent color. No cool or saturated artificial lighting anywhere in the scene — grade stays warm and naturalistic.

WARDROBE
Yuki's seifuku and Ren's blazer stay clean, no tears or damage — this is a schoolyard scuffle, not a brawl. Fabric moves naturally with each motion.

AUDIO
Footsteps on wood, fabric rustle, controlled impact thuds, one soft laugh from Yuki before her line. No music.

STYLE
Ultra-photorealistic live-action, naturalistic Japanese school setting, realistic hair and fabric physics, natural skin texture, physically accurate contact shadows, subtle motion blur on fast moves, 8K detail, shallow depth of field.

OUTPUT SETTINGS
16:9. Real-time throughout except the controlled slow-motion window specified in the 0:02–0:04 beat, which snaps back to real-time on impact.

POSITIVE LOCKS
@yuki keeps the same face, hairstyle, uniform, and tie throughout. @ren and the two other upperclassmen keep consistent uniforms and builds across every cut. The stepladder remains a stepladder — no prop transformation. No teleportation between beats; every position change follows visible, continuous motion. No blood, no visible injury, no exaggerated magic effects.
```

## 71. Seedance: Yoriichi vs. the Hundred Demons

```text
无限城 BGM 一响，
缘一 VS 百鬼  ⚔️💥
纯粹的战斗爽感！

Seedance 2.5 
Prompt 🔽

【基础设定】

30秒，16:9。暗色幻想绘画动画风格，粗粝厚涂笔触、硬边明暗、概括色块、清晰人物剪影。

人物、敌群、建筑、武器统一为具有立体空间感的绘画动画。

高速运动中保持人物、刀锋、命中位置清楚。

头发、衣摆、碎木、灰烬具有真实惯性与重量。

整体色调以墨黑、灰青、旧木褐为主，人物暗红服装作为主要识别色。

【参考图绑定】
图片1= 战场建筑与空间结构参考。
仅采用悬空木平台、栏杆、回廊、阶梯、大殿及上下交错建筑关系。
图片2 = 敌群外观参考。
提取不同体型、皮肤、眼睛、獠牙、长爪、破旧衣物与异化轮廓，扩展为高密度敌潮。
图片3= 主角唯一身份与服装参考。
锁定脸型、长发、暗红外衣、敞领、腰带、宽裤、绑腿与鞋履。

【角色规则】

全片始终只有一名主角。
始终使用同一把实体刀，刀鞘固定腰侧。

右手主握，重斩时左手辅助同一刀柄。

人物面部克制，视线始终寻找下一处敌阵缺口。

所有攻击必须由真实身体动作驱动：

蹬地 → 转髋 → 挥刀 → 回腕 → 连续落步。

高速移动过程中人物本体始终连续可追踪。

禁止瞬移。

禁止实体分身。

禁止第二把刀。

【敌群规则】

敌人数百只。

中央平台、两侧回廊、远端阶梯与建筑阴影始终存在大量敌人。

前景使用肩背、头部、爪子压住画面边缘。

中景多排敌人包围人物移动路线。

背景敌群一直延伸至建筑深处。

敌人必须持续：

扑入、夹击、绕侧、封路、补位。

人物移动到哪里，新的敌群就从前方与侧翼压入。

人物经过之后才允许留下局部缺口。

24秒之前禁止大面积清空战场。

【核心战斗逻辑】

模式：

流光割草 + 极限连招密度。

全片以：

高速穿群
移动旋斩
横扫
反手斩
斜劈
短跃下劈

作为主要动作。

目标约26组短连招。

每组动作必须：

起手 → 接招 → 收招，无缝连接。

上一刀的收刀方向，直接成为下一组攻击的起始方向。

禁止攻击后站住重新摆姿势。

禁止三刀打一组然后停顿。

人物始终边打边移动。
【空间规则】
主平台远端阶梯 = 北。
入口 = 南。
左右两侧分别为东、西。
人物移动路线：

南部
→ 东侧
→ 中央
→ 西侧
→ 北部。

摄影机始终朝向尚未清除的敌群推进。

人物身前必须持续存在新的敌阵。

身后逐渐形成已经战斗过的路径。

空间、建筑地标、人物方向必须保持连续。

【镜头结构】

全片10个镜头段。

每段约3秒。

Shot 1｜0–3s

Wide，高位斜俯视追踪。

人物直接高速切入右侧敌群。

突入横斩
→ 反手回切
→ 移动回旋
→ 再次斜切进入下一排。

没有登场。

没有拔刀。

没有蓄力。
第一秒直接进入战斗。
Shot 2｜3–6s
FS，贴地侧前跟拍。
人物沿东侧战区高速向北推进。

连续横扫、反手斩、斜劈。

敌人从正面和侧面不断补入。

摄影机倒退跟拍，同时保留前方大量敌群。

Shot 3｜6–9s

Wide，升高斜俯视追踪。

人物从东侧切向平台中央。

连续三组移动旋斩。

每一次旋转都改变人物所在位置。

禁止原地转圈。

镜头展示人物身后逐渐形成的战斗路线。

Shot 4｜9–12s

FS，低位跟进。
人物进入中央最密集敌阵。
横扫
→ 反撩
→ 斜切
→ 上挑
→ 踏进压斩
→ 短跃下劈。
上层回廊与地面敌人同时压入。

人物必须同时应对上下两个方向。

Shot 5｜12–15s

FS，侧面短弧环绕。

<details>
<summary>Show full prompt</summary>

```text
SEEDANCE 2.5 | TEXT TO VIDEO | SINGLE CONTINUOUS TAKE | 22s | 1080x1920 | 9:16 | 30fps | 1080p

SCENE AND LOCATION
A hidden volcanic spring canyon deep in a Pacific Northwest pine forest on a bright summer day. A narrow, deep gorge with steep, very high walls of dark gray basalt made of vertical hexagonal columns, streaked with tan and rust tones and covered in patches of bright green moss, ferns and small shrubs.
At the bottom is a crystal-clear natural spring pool. The shallow edges glow bright turquoise and aqua, fading to deep sapphire blue in the center. The water is so transparent that the bottom is fully visible: honeycomb-patterned volcanic rock, mossy boulders, and two long pale submerged logs lying diagonally across the bottom.
Two white waterfalls pour into the pool: a thin one at the far back of the canyon and a larger one spilling from the right-hand cliff wall, making white foam where it hits the water. Tall dark-green conifer and pine trees line the canyon rim. Above is a vivid clear blue sky with a few small white clouds. Hard, bright midday sunlight with strong contrast, and sun sparkles on the water.

THE JUMPER
A young man seen only from his own first-person view. Tanned bare feet and lightly hairy shins, and loose navy-blue swim shorts with a large mustard-yellow hibiscus flower pattern and white drawstrings. No shirt, no shoes, no gloves. His hands and forearms appear naturally in frame when he moves them.

CAMERA AND REALISM
First-person POV from a GoPro strapped to his forehead. It looks like real action-cam footage, not a cinematic camera. 16mm-equivalent ultra-wide lens with slight barrel distortion, and saturated but natural color.
Use real action-cam behavior throughout: small head bob and micro-jitter from breathing and body weight, rolling-shutter wobble on fast moves, quick auto-exposure shifts between bright sky and the shaded canyon, a small sun flare on upward tilts, light sensor noise in the shadows, and wind buffeting that shakes the frame during the fall.
The cliff is about 35 meters high. Gravity, body motion and water physics are fully realistic.

0-2s | THE EDGE (real time)
POV looking straight down from the top of the basalt cliff. His bare feet stand on the rough, dark rock ledge at the bottom of frame, with the shorts visible at the lower edge. Both hands hover forward at the sides of frame. The turquoise pool, the submerged logs and the waterfalls are far, far below. His toes curl over the edge and small grit crumbles off. His right hand tosses a pink-and-white striped inflatable tube out over the drop; it tumbles down and lands as a tiny speck on the water far below. Wind, distant waterfall roar, upbeat chill electronic music starts.

2-4s | THE COUNTDOWN (real time)
A quick nervous tilt down at the drop, a sharp exhale, then two fast steps back from the edge, with the ledge bouncing in frame. Young male voice, hyped: "Three, two, one!"

4-7s | THE TAKEOFF (natural slow motion, about 50% speed)
Two running steps forward, then his last foot pushes off the ledge in slow motion. Rock grit kicks up and floats in the sunlight, the cliff edge slides away beneath his feet, his arms swing forward and out wide, and the whole turquoise canyon opens up below. The slow motion looks like a real high-frame-rate action-cam clip, not a frozen hang. Deep bass swell, stretched wind.

7-13s | THE FALL (real time, fast and continuous)
The speed ramps smoothly back to normal and the stomach drops.
7-9s: The frame dips and wobbles, his arms flail slightly for balance at the edges of frame, and his feet kick at the bottom of frame. A loud yell: "Woooooo!"
9-11s: The basalt column wall, moss and ferns streak upward past the lens, and the right-side waterfall rushes by with spray catching the sun. Wind roars louder and the frame shakes from air buffeting.
11-13s: The glowing turquoise water rushes up and fills the frame, and the pink tube and submerged logs grow fast. His arms snap tight to his body and his legs lock straight for a feet-first pencil dive. The music builds to the drop.

13-14s | IMPACT
A hard feet-first hit into the water and a white explosion of bubbles swallows the lens. Loud plunge splash, deep impact whoosh, and the music goes heavily muffled underwater.

14-17s | UNDERWATER (short, energetic, real time)
A violent swirl of silver bubbles clears to reveal crystal-clear blue water, bright sun shafts cutting down from the surface, the pale submerged logs and honeycomb volcanic rock bottom below, and small fish darting away. His hands push into frame for one strong breaststroke pull, then fast kicks straight up toward the bright glowing surface, with bubbles streaming past the lens. No slow sinking.

17-19s | SURFACE BREAK
The camera bursts through the surface, water droplets sliding across the lens, and bright sunlight floods in. The music snaps back to full clarity on the beat drop. His hand grabs the pink-and-white tube floating right beside him.

19-22s | THE REWARD
POV bobbing on the turquoise water holding the tube, then a sharp tilt up revealing the towering basalt cliff, the tiny ledge he jumped from far above, the pine trees on the rim and the vivid blue sky, with the waterfall splashing on the right. Same voice, out of breath and triumphant: "Yeah, dude! That was insane!" Faint distant cheering. Music plays clean to the final frame.

AUDIO
Continuous upbeat chill electronic track: a bass swell on takeoff, a fast build through the fall, muffled underwater, then a clean drop on surfacing. Real ambient layers: wind that grows with speed, fabric flapping, heavy breathing, waterfall roar, splash, bubbles. Dialogue clear over the music.

KEEP
One continuous take, first-person only. The same shorts, feet and hands throughout, and the same canyon layout with two waterfalls, turquoise pool and submerged logs. The slow motion lasts only 3 seconds at takeoff. The fall is fast, real time and builds speed without pausing. Every beat is quick and punchy.

AVOID
Extra slow motion beyond the takeoff, hovering or floating mid-air, smooth cinematic gimbal movement, cuts to third person, showing the jumper's face, extra people, distorted hands or extra fingers, morphing cliffs, changing water color, text, subtitles, watermarks.
```

【核心规则】

1.零延迟衔接：招式与招式之间零缓冲，一击未落、下一击已出，剑刃始终保持高频接触。
2.动作驱动场景转换（关键）：任何场景变化（柱身→地面→横梁→大殿）必须由人物的具体动作完成——例如"男主被震退撞向殿柱""女主借力从柱顶翻身跃下砸向地面""两人缠斗中撞穿一排烛台滚落台阶"——绝不允许镜头单独切换场景而人物动作不连续。
3.真实力量感：每一次剑刃相击都要有清晰的身体位移反馈——被格挡的一方手臂震退半步、脚下打滑、身体因冲击力扭转——不是"两人站定互砍"，而是每一下都带着真实的体重和冲力。
4.禁止空转镜头：任何时候镜头运动（推近、环绕、甩镜）必须跟随人物正在进行的具体肢体动作，禁止画面出现人物静止而镜头单独旋转/环绕的情况。
5.贴身险招库：全片穿插以下具体招式，且不重复：
•贴地躲闪：一方横扫斩来，另一方猛然后仰几乎贴地，剑锋堪堪掠过鼻尖，发丝被削断
•直刺反击：躲闪的同时反手直刺，剑尖笔直突刺而出
•旋身格挡：被直刺方原地急速旋身躲开，同时手腕翻转格挡下一击
•翻滚起身反击：被打退跌地的一方就地翻滚，借旋转之力直接刺出反击
•剑锋绞缠近身：两剑绞在一起后，双方近距离肩靠肩角力，忽然同时脱手推开重新拉开架势

【分镜控制 0:00–0:30，全程动作连续无跳切】

[0:00–0:02] 35mm广角，低角度地面轨道镜头，紧跟人物冲刺
女主与男主同时从大殿两端全速冲刺而来，在殿中央剑刃相击的瞬间发力对撞，冲击力震得两人各自后退半步、脚下地砖迸裂溅起碎屑——开场即是全力对撞，无任何铺垫。

[0:02–0:07] 50mm中景转特写，镜头紧贴人物身体运动，快速手持跟拍
近战组1：男主大幅横扫斩来，女主猛然后仰几乎贴地躲避，剑锋堪堪从她鼻尖上方划过，发丝被削断飘散；女主借后仰反弹之力顺势翻身站起同时反手直刺男主胸前；男主急速旋身躲开的同时手腕翻转格挡，两剑绞在一起，双方肩靠肩角力两秒后同时发力震开，各自后退两步喘息。

[0:07–0:09] 85mm长焦跟拍，人物动作驱动的场景转换
男主被震开的力道带得撞向身后龙柱，借柱身反弹之力翻身跃起，脚踏盘龙浮雕垂直跑上柱身；女主紧接着一个爆发式起跳追上柱身，两人在柱身上剑刃相击两三招，剑刃擦过柱身留下灼痕。

[0:09–0:12] 长焦跟拍转俯冲镜头，人物从高处坠落带动场景切换
男主被逼到柱顶横梁边缘，女主一记重击将他震退坠落，男主借坠落之势在半空中翻身调整姿态，剑尖朝下如同俯冲攻击，重重落回大殿地面的同时挥剑横扫；女主几乎同时从柱身跃下正面接下这一击，两剑相撞火花四溅，双方手臂因冲击力明显震颤——此处完成从柱身到大殿地面的自然场景过渡，全程动作不中断。

[0:12–0:17] 50mm特写，镜头持续跟随人物肢体动作，无空镜转场
近战组2（决战前最密集回合）：女主连续三次变向斩击，速度一次比一次快——男主第一次侧身避开、第二次贴地躲过、第三次干脆矮身钻入她剑锋下方近身，反手横斩女主腰侧；女主凌空后翻堪堪躲开，落地瞬间衣摆被剑气削开一角；男主趁她落地瞬间欺身直刺，女主侧身堪堪避开剑尖擦过肩侧，衣料应声裂开，同时反手一记重击震得男主剑身发出金属哀鸣、虎口震裂似地后退两步。

[0:17–0:21] 超广角跟拍，两人剧烈缠斗中撞向环境
两人剑刃绞缠近身角力，忽然同时脱手震开各自后退，紧接着几乎同时再度暴起冲向对方——这一冲带倒了身旁一排烛台，烛火被撞灭又被剑气余波重新点燃，两人在倒地的烛台与四溅的烛油中继续近身缠斗，剑刃碰撞产生密集火花，地面积水被踏起层层水雾。

[0:21–0:26] 特写转全景交替，剪辑跟随攻防节奏（非空转镜头）
双方招式速度达到全片顶点：斩、刺、格挡、反击在两三秒内连续发生，每一次碰撞都有清晰的身体反馈——女主被震得踉跄半步随即反手补上一剑，男主格挡后手臂剧烈震颤仍强行反击；蓝绿剑气缠绕成螺旋光流，两人的呼吸声与兵刃声交织成密集的节奏感，全程无一帧人物静止。

[0:26–0:29] 超广角全景，剧烈镜头震动，跟随最终对撞
双方在剑锋交汇的瞬间同时使出全力一击，两股能量对撞爆发出席卷全殿的蓝绿交织光潮，冲击波将两人同时震飞后退数米，地砖与柱身被震裂，烛火大片熄灭，烟尘弥漫整个神殿，镜头剧烈震颤后画面渐趋失焦泛白。

[0:29–0:30] 固定机位，画面渐缓
烟雾缓缓弥散，两人分别单膝或站立稳住身形，胸口剧烈起伏、握剑的手因手臂力竭仍在颤抖，残存的烛火星星点点重新亮起，画面定格于两人对峙的宏大空镜。

【通用限制】

Never redesign faces. Never change costumes. Never replace the temple. Never introduce additional characters. No face drift. No costume drift. No environment drift. No duplicated people. No dialogue. No narration. No subtitles. No text. No watermark. 全程镜头必须跟随人物具体动作运动，禁止人物静止时镜头单独旋转或空转。
```

Core Theme
University field, one take fight scene. Two fighters, hand to hand combat with real stunts and kicks. Fight ends with the girl's fire power awakening. She finishes him with a decisive final blow. Continuous shot, no cuts, no edits. 16:9 aspect ratio.

Character Appearance Lock

FIGHTER A (Boy)
Asian male, around 20 years old. Short black hair, slightly covering forehead. Almond-shaped brown eyes, defined jawline. Fitted white athletic tank top. Yellow lemon-colored shorts, black sneakers. Confident, relaxed posture, athletic build.

FIGHTER B (Girl)
Japanese female, early 20s, striking model-like features. Lean, tall, athletic figure. Silver-lavender hair, chin length bob with straight fringe. Sharp, focused amber eyes. Pink acrylic short skirt, high-shine finish. Cropped white top. Pink knee-high socks, white sneakers. Fast, agile, aggressive fighting style — real kicks, spins, and strikes.

Environment Lock
University open ground, trimmed grass field with dirt patches. White painted field lines. Overhead campus lamp posts casting pools of light. Everything outside the light pools is deep shadow. University buildings faintly visible in the background darkness. Dust and grass kick up on every impact, roll, and landing.

Timeline

0.0–2.5s: Fighter A stands behind Fighter B. Right palm covers her mouth from behind. She grabs at his wrist, eyes sharp and furious. She drives her elbow into his ribs, breaks free, spins out. She lands in a fighting stance, dust rising.

2.5–5.0s: She rolls her shoulders, cracks her neck. Walks forward with slow, aggressive confidence. Speaks clearly in English to camera: "You really thought you could catch me off guard?"

5.0–7.0s: He sprints forward, throws a right hook. She ducks low, sweeps his front leg. He stumbles, catches balance, resets stance.

7.0–9.0s: He throws a straight right jab. She parries with her left forearm, steps in. Delivers a sharp right knee strike to his side. He staggers back two steps.

9.0–12.5s: He lands a glancing punch on her shoulder. She barely reacts, grabs his wrist, twists. Executes a real hip-throw, slams him to the ground. He rolls once, pushes up onto one knee, breathing hard.

12.5–16.0s: Camera pushes in on her face. Sweat, dirt, sharp determined eyes. First orange-red flame threads flicker along her fingertips. Threads crawl up her arms to her shoulders. Her eyes begin to glow amber-orange from within. The lamp posts start flickering irregularly.

16.0–18.0s: She exhales sharply, rolls her wrists. Small flames bloom across both palms. Fabric of her top singes faintly at the edges. Heat distortion ripples the air around her.

18.0–21.0s: Camera circles her fast. He rises, throws a desperate punch. She sidesteps, grabs his arm, flame licking her grip. Grass scorches in a ring beneath her feet. Flame trails follow every movement of her limbs.

21.0–23.0s: She unleashes a flurry — spinning back kick, followed by a jumping roundhouse. Each strike leaves a brief trail of fire in the air. He blocks two, takes the third across the jaw. He drops to one knee, dazed, smoke rising off his shoulder.

23.0–27.5s: She plants her feet, pulls both fists back, flames coiling tight around her forearms. Steps in with full aggression, drives a flaming knee strike upward into his chest. Impact releases a burst of fire and sparks outward.

27.5–30.0s: He's launched back, lands hard, skidding through dirt. She stands over him, flames dying down her arms to embers. Breathing hard, one fist still smoking. Lamp posts dim to near darkness. Only the fading orange glow lights her face. Then everything goes dark.

Lighting
Extremely dark night, deep shadows. Lamp posts as rim light on shoulders and hair. Warm orange-red glow after her power appears, casting light on both fighters.

Sound Design
Location sound only. Grass and dirt scraping, fabric rustling. Sharp impact sounds, heavy breathing, grunts. Flame ignition crackle building to a low roar. Lamp buzz as they flicker. Her line delivered clearly at 2.5–5.0s. Sharp exhale and crackle at the final blow. Total silence at the very end. No background music throughout.
```

## 114. Combat Medic Rescues Wounded Soldiers on Okinawa

▶ **[Watch video](https://cdn.lipsync.video/lipsync/cdn/feature-case/ea68c05a-2aff-4186-8760-67e77f00c6c0/generate.mp4)**

**16:9 · 720p · 30s · Seedance**

[Original post](https://x.com/Diplomeme/status/2100458083831234699) · 2026-09-17 13:33 (UTC+08:00)

<details>
<summary>Show full prompt</summary>

```text
30 seconds | 16:9 | hyper-realistic historical war-film recreation | documentary realism | MULTISHOT

HISTORICAL DRAMATIZATION — An original cinematic reconstruction inspired by the Battle of Okinawa in 1945. The story follows a fictional U.S. Army combat medic attempting to evacuate wounded soldiers from an exposed battlefield. Do not recreate any specific scene, character, dialogue, costume design, camera composition, or sequence from any existing film.

CORE CONCEPT
A young combat medic remains behind after his unit moves forward. Instead of becoming part of the fighting, he moves through smoke, mud and artillery fire searching for wounded soldiers and bringing them toward safety. The sequence emphasizes courage, exhaustion, confusion and the physical reality of a battlefield rather than glorifying combat.

SETTING
Okinawa, Japan — spring 1945. Rugged limestone escarpment, muddy defensive positions, damaged vegetation, smoke-filled battlefield, earthworks, abandoned equipment, scattered ammunition crates, military stretchers and distant artillery. Overcast tropical sky gradually brightening toward morning.

CHARACTERS
MAIN MEDIC — Fictional American Army combat medic, mid-20s, exhausted face, short regulation haircut, olive-drab 1940s combat uniform, steel helmet, canvas medical bag, field dressings and worn boots.

WOUNDED SOLDIER 1 — Young American infantryman, exhausted and injured, period-correct uniform and equipment.

WOUNDED SOLDIER 2 — Another fictional infantryman trapped farther across the exposed ground.

BACKGROUND SOLDIERS — Period-accurate American and Japanese forces visible only as distant silhouettes through smoke. No identifiable real individuals.

STORY — MULTISHOT VISUALS

00:00–02.5 — DAWN
24mm wide establishing shot of an Okinawa battlefield covered in smoke and mist. Exhausted American soldiers move between muddy defensive positions while distant artillery flashes across the horizon. MAIN MEDIC stands alone near a damaged defensive position, scanning the battlefield.

02.5–05 — DISCOVERY
50mm handheld tracking shot as the medic hears a faint voice. He moves through smoke and discovers WOUNDED SOLDIER 1 lying behind a collapsed section of earthwork. The medic immediately kneels and checks him.

05–07.5 — FIRST AID
85mm intimate close-up of the medic applying a field dressing. His hands shake slightly from exhaustion. Mud and sweat cover his face. Distant artillery causes the ground to tremble.

07.5–10 — CARRY
35mm handheld shot. The medic pulls the wounded soldier onto his shoulders and begins moving through the mud. His boots sink into the wet ground. He struggles under the weight but continues.

10–12.5 — INCOMING FIRE
A distant artillery impact throws dirt into the air nearby. The medic instinctively drops low, shielding the wounded soldier with his body. Smoke rolls across the frame.

12.5–15 — HEAR ANOTHER VOICE
The medic looks toward a distant voice coming through the smoke. He realizes another wounded soldier is still behind him. After a brief moment of hesitation, he turns around and moves back.

15–17.5 — SECOND CASUALTY
50mm handheld movement through dense smoke. The medic reaches WOUNDED SOLDIER 2, who is trapped beside damaged equipment. The medic pulls debris away and checks his condition.

17.5–20 — IMPROVISED STRETCHER
35mm close tracking shot. The medic uses a canvas sheet and available wooden poles to construct a crude field stretcher. His movements are rushed but practical and believable.

20–22.5 — EXTRACTION
Wide 24mm shot. The medic drags the improvised stretcher across exposed muddy ground. Smoke obscures the battlefield behind him while artillery flashes intermittently in the distance.

22.5–25 — THE ESCARPMENT
The medic reaches the edge of the rocky escarpment. He lowers himself carefully and begins moving the wounded soldier toward a safer lower position.

25–27.5 — HUMAN MOMENT
85mm close-up. The wounded soldier looks up at the medic. The medic briefly reassures him with a calm expression before continuing. No heroic speech, no exaggerated emotion.

27.5–30 — FINAL REVEAL
24mm wide shot from behind the medic. He helps both wounded soldiers toward a sheltered position while the enormous Okinawa battlefield stretches behind them through smoke and morning light. The camera holds on the scale of the battlefield as the three figures become small against the landscape.

CAMERA
Large-format historical cinema camera language combined with embedded war-correspondent realism. 24mm for battlefield scale, 35mm for movement and action, 50mm for human interaction, 85mm for intimate details. Handheld shoulder-mounted movement, natural camera shake, imperfect framing, realistic focus changes, atmospheric obstruction from smoke and dust. No modern drone aesthetic, no impossible camera movement.

LIGHTING
Soft overcast Okinawa morning light, cold gray atmosphere, muted sunlight through smoke, warm distant artillery flashes, natural skin highlights and subtle atmospheric haze. No theatrical spotlighting.

PHYSICS
Realistic body weight, fatigue, mud resistance, stretcher weight, smoke movement, artillery shockwaves, falling debris, clothing movement and ground interaction. The medic struggles physically under the wounded soldier’s weight. No superhuman strength or impossible movement.

HUMAN PERFORMANCE
Exhaustion is visible in posture, breathing and movement. The medic behaves professionally but is visibly frightened and tired. Wounded soldiers respond naturally to pain and shock. Background soldiers move independently and react to distant impacts without synchronized choreography.

AUDIO
Authentic battlefield soundscape: distant artillery, rifle fire far across the battlefield, shouting, heavy breathing, boots in mud, equipment rattling, stretcher fabric stretching, wind through vegetation and debris impacts. Music is extremely restrained and secondary to environmental sound.

VISUAL CHARACTER
Photorealistic live-action historical war cinematography. Natural skin texture, authentic 1945 uniforms, weathered equipment, mud, sweat, smoke, atmospheric depth, realistic film grain and subtle highlight halation. No plastic skin, no videogame appearance, no exaggerated color grading.

COLOR GRADE
Muted olive greens, dirty browns, gray-blue atmospheric shadows, warm skin tones and restrained highlights. Slightly desaturated period-war-film palette while maintaining natural colors.

HISTORICAL REALISM
Period-correct American military equipment, clothing, medical supplies, terrain and battlefield environment. No modern weapons, vehicles, helmets, medical equipment, tactical gear or technology.

CONTINUITY
MAIN MEDIC, both wounded soldiers, uniforms, equipment, medical bag, weather and battlefield geography remain consistent across all shots. Wounds remain visually consistent without graphic gore. The sequence progresses logically from discovery → treatment → evacuation → second rescue → extraction.

FINAL QUALITY TARGET
The footage should feel like an extraordinarily realistic 1945 battlefield documentary captured by a professional war cinematographer, with the emotional intensity of a major historical film but without reproducing any existing movie scene. The realism comes from human performance, physical exhaustion, practical battlefield detail, smoke, mud, sound and environmental scale.
```

</details>

【摄影】

PYONA倒地后摄影机立刻降低到接近木地板的高度，同时捕捉她的疼痛表情、周围女生嘲笑和敌人的腿部位置。

LOW WATER SPIN SWEEP这一个动作的摄影机不要高速绕着PYONA旋转，也不要跟着她向前移动。使用低机位、稍宽的侧前方视角，在PYONA扫腿核心动作的0.8–1.0秒内保持稳定清晰，让同一画面能够读到：
PYONA固定撑地手 + 低位髋部旋转 + 伸直扫腿 + 第一名敌人腿部被命中 + 第二名敌人随后被扫中。
不要用慢动作表现这次扫腿。
HYDRO STEP使用快速跟拍。
WATER GUARD中近景拍清拳头撞入水膜。
FACE WATER CANNON必须看见水从PYONA掌前直接轰中敌人脸部。
RING WATER SHOCKWAVE使用稍宽镜头，同时看见PYONA、围攻者和环形水压向外爆开。
最后HYDRAULIC PALM进入强烈中近景，优先展示身体接触、衣料压缩、水压爆发和敌人反应。
ACTION READABILITY > PHYSICAL IMPACT > WATER POWER > CAMERA CHAOS.
【绝对禁止】
禁止第一镜头只拍PYONA背影。
禁止重新觉醒。
禁止PYONA研究自己的水能力。
禁止开场未完整摔倒就反击。
禁止LOW WATER SPIN SWEEP变成：
slow slide、
slide tackle、
forward crawl、
forward travel、
slow spin、
breakdance、
dance move、
slow-motion spinning attack。
禁止PYONA在扫腿时向敌人方向滑过去。
禁止PYONA身体中心在扫腿过程中明显向前移动。
禁止轻轻碰腿后敌人才慢慢倒下。
禁止敌人在没有明确腿部接触的情况下自动摔倒。
禁止PYONA轻轻挥手后敌人自动摔倒。
禁止任何谨慎、轻柔、慢吞吞的格斗。
禁止敌人排队。
禁止敌人受击后没有明显身体反应。
禁止18秒站着长时间聚水。
禁止FACE WATER CANNON像轻柔喷水。
禁止23秒生成静态水结界或保护罩。
禁止巨大海啸、水龙、魔法阵、激光、能量球、瞬移。
禁止攻击篮球架、器材或空气。
禁止游戏CG人物、廉价动漫特效、AI塑料皮肤。
禁止英雄Pose收尾。
核心目标：
开场必须清楚看见PYONA的脸。她被正面重拳击中腹部后真实、完整地摔倒，并被周围女生大声嘲笑。
第一反击必须严格表现为原地低位圆形扫腿，而不是任何形式的滑行或冲刺。
PYONA仍然倒在原来的位置，一只手牢牢撑地，以该支撑点和低位髋部为轴，一条腿完全伸直，在约0.8–1.0秒内完成爆发式高速圆形横扫。她的身体中心基本保持原位，真正移动的是高速旋转的腿。腿先真实扫中第一名敌人的脚踝/小腿，使其支撑腿瞬间失效并重重摔倒；同一次旋转立即继续扫中第二名敌人，使其同样失去下盘并重摔。
水只能贴着PYONA真实扫腿轨迹增强速度、冲击和横向力量，绝不能代替腿部接触自动把人打倒。
除此之外，后续动作保持原有结构不变：
Hydro Step高速突进 → Water Guard硬接攻击 → Face Water Cannon正面强力轰击 → Ring Water Shockwave把多人向外猛烈震退 → Hydraulic Palm以真实掌部接触结合极高水压完成高潮。

整场动作必须强硬、快速、危险、毫不克制，绝不能呈现谨慎、温柔或慢吞吞的动作感觉。
```

结束时：Dust cloud rolling across the empty demolition zone, the demolition foreman checking his clipboard confused, the helicopter hovering proudly above.

阶段四
承接上一阶段：Identical continuous helicopter shot, no cut, same aircraft and pilot.
主要事件：A police negotiation helicopter rises to hover at a careful distance, an officer with a megaphone pleading. 阶段五

The camera may whip, orbit, dive, roll, pass behind objects, move overhead, go low to the ground, rush toward her, or briefly lose sight of her behind foreground elements, but it must remain one physically continuous shot.

Character: A realistic Japanese girl in her early 20s. Natural features, long dark hair, athletic and expressive. Casual modern clothes that stay readable as they get wet, dusty, torn, and transformed by each world. Photoreal, not anime.

Start in a bright Candy World. Giant lollipops, frosting hills, candy-cane trees, and sugar dust in the air. The girl hears a rumble and spins around. The camera rushes backward in front of her as she runs toward a gigantic painting of a lava cake. She skids to a stop. Molten chocolate lava suddenly explodes out of the canvas. She dives sideways as real lava-cake flood crashes across the candy floor. The camera drops almost to floor level with the rushing chocolate. She grabs a giant candy bench to stop herself. The bench stretches beneath her hands and becomes a fallen log. The camera swings around her. She is suddenly in the Year of the Dinosaurs, hanging from a tree as a massive dinosaur charges past. She pulls herself up, runs, jumps over a sliding boulder, ducks beneath a swinging vine, then grabs a giant leaf. The camera rises with the leaf. The leaf wraps around her. She tears through it and tumbles onto a paper world. The same leaf is now a huge sheet of folded paper. She rolls across paper mountains, pushes herself up, and immediately begins running as paper buildings unfold around her. The camera races sideways with her. Paper edges whip past the lens and transform into glass. The paper city becomes a modern skyscraper corridor. She runs across glossy floors as windows race past. She loses her footing near an open shaft and falls. The camera dives with her. The glass walls become rough stone. She lands hard inside a prehistoric BC cave. Torches flicker. She crawls forward, then sprints as the cave opens onto an ancient battlefield. Spears and banners rush past. She drops flat as riders leap over her. From ground level a giant war banner falls toward the lens. She grabs its edge. The wind yanks her upward. For a moment she is lifted completely off the ground. As the camera rotates with her, the banner becomes the feathers of a gigantic bird. She is suddenly riding on the big bird’s back high in the sky. She holds on as it banks through clouds. The bird dives. She slips, grabs a talon, then drops. The camera falls with her. She plunges into an underground river. Now underwater, her momentum continues naturally. She swims with the current through a dark cavern, then breaks the surface onto an abandoned island. Skeletons lie scattered across the sand and rocks. She stands, panting, then steps on a glass shard. The island shrinks around her. The camera pulls back as the whole island is revealed inside a giant glass bottle. She is trapped inside the bottle. The bottle tips. She slides, slams against the glass, and the glass becomes a dusty window. She crashes through it into a creepy house. Creaking floors, peeling wallpaper, long hallways. She runs. A door slams open. The house corridors fill with a zombie world. She dodges reaching hands, leaps over fallen furniture, and bursts through a classroom door. She is suddenly in a school classroom. Desks scatter as she sprints across them and jumps through an open window. The camera follows her through the window. She does not land in a yard. She lands on the top of a building. Wind slams her. She runs across the rooftop, then slips off the edge. The camera dives beside her. She hits water. Now underwater again. She swims upward toward a bright shape. When she breaks the surface, she is pulling herself into a pilot’s cockpit of a flying plane. Instruments shake. She grabs the controls, then the plane punches through clouds into a dense forest. The cockpit becomes tree branches. She tumbles through leaves, rolls across moss, and slams onto a mattress.

Instantly she is sitting upright in bed. Morning. Silence. The camera is now extremely close to her face. She gasps awake. For the first time in the entire film, everything becomes completely still. Her bedroom is ordinary. She looks around. She touches the mattress. Checks her hands. Relieved, she lies back down. Then something drips onto her forehead. She looks up. The ceiling above her bed is gently rippling like candy glaze mixed with water. A large shadow slowly moves across it. Her expression changes from relief to disbelief. Then the entire bed suddenly tilts. She grabs the mattress as her alarm clock, pillow, lamp, and furniture begin sliding toward one side of the room. End exactly as she begins sliding out of bed, suggesting that waking up was only another stage of the transformation.

The action should feel choreographed like a physical adventure. The girl must run, sprint, skid, dive, fall, climb, hang, swing, crawl, jump, roll, swim, land, sit, get thrown through spaces, and briefly become weightless. Her position within the frame should constantly change: close-up, full-body, profile, silhouette, overhead, low-angle, behind her, directly in front of her, underwater, upside down, falling beside her, and orbiting around her.

Transitions should be triggered by her actions. When she grabs something, it becomes something else. When she falls, the next world catches her. When she jumps through an opening, that opening becomes another location. When an object crosses the camera, it transforms while hiding part of the environment.

Use physical match transitions such as:
candy bench → dinosaur-era log
giant leaf → paper sheet
paper edges → skyscraper glass
falling shaft → BC cave stone
war banner → giant bird feathers
bird dive → underground river
island sand → bottle glass
bottle glass → creepy house window
house door → zombie world
zombie corridor → classroom
classroom window → rooftop
rooftop fall → underwater
water surface → pilot cockpit
cockpit / clouds → forest branches
forest ground → bedroom mattress

The camera movement itself should help generate transitions. A whip-pan can turn one moving object into another. A 360-degree orbit can reveal a completely transformed environment by the time it completes. Passing behind fabric, pillars, people, waves, vehicles, doors, or the girl’s body can briefly obscure parts of the frame while the transformation happens continuously.

Despite the speed, every transformation must remain visually understandable. The result should feel less like a transformation montage and more like one impossible 30-second physical journey in which the girl is being chased, thrown, carried, dropped, and pulled through realities that continuously grow out of one another.

Tone: exhilarating, mysterious, playful, cinematic, photoreal, large-scale, dreamlike, and full of wonder.
```

</details>
